- **Apply now:** 333 open, not yet applied: 198 in your areas, 135 elsewhere (49 with a stated deadline)
- **Referrals / cold messages now:** 235 expected to open by 2026-11-30 or overdue vs last year
- **Tracker leads to confirm:** 0 listed by a GitHub tracker, company board not checked yet
- **Later / unknown timing:** 256
- **Already in TRACKER.md:** 40
- **No US new-grad SWE track found:** 863
- **Dropped (citizenship, green card, or clearance required):** 95 — kept in `status.csv` as `excluded`

"Apply now" only counts postings seen on the company's own job board. GitHub trackers (SimplifyJobs, speedyapply, vanshb03) are used for leads and last-cycle dates. Workday dates are the latest repost.
